We present the most comprehensive analysis to date of MLP activation-based deep active learning methods applied to in-context learning, including how different attention masking strategies impact active learning across diverse classification and generative datasets, using both Llama-3.2-3B and Qwen2.5-3B base models. However, we find a negative result: MLP outputs, viewed through the lenses of massive activations or the first four moments, do not correlate with example quality or task performance. Specifically, the absolute Spearman correlation coefficient is at most 0.33 for all tasks and models we tested, showing that such activation-based sampling should not be used for in-context learning. We hypothesise that this may be due to superposition, whereby models represent more features than they have dimensionality, suggesting that methods like Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) may be a promising future direction.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [80] [MaskForge: Structure-Aware Adaptive Attacks for Jailbreaking Diffusion Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04027)
*Yingzi Ma,Zhengyue Zhao,Xiaogeng Liu,Minhui Xue,Yue Zhao,Chaowei Xiao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MaskForge是一种针对扩散大语言模型（dLLM）的黑盒自适应攻击方法，它通过优化搜索结构化模式的库来实现红队测试，在多个dLLM和基准上平均攻击成功率达到79.3%，相比最强基线提升17.6%，并具有跨目标迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型(dLLM)通过双向上下文迭代去噪部分掩码序列来生成文本，其安全暴露面与自回归LLM不同。现有越狱方法要么忽略了dLLM原生的填充能力，要么使用低多样性的掩码模板均匀应用于所有目标，缺乏结构适应性和攻击经验积累。

Method: MaskForge将dLLM红队测试建模为对不断增长的结构模式库的优化搜索。它将成功攻击尝试抽象为可复用的模式（schema），使用UCB bandit选择与目标兼容的模式，当当前库失败时调用评分器引导的备用策略。成功尝试被蒸馏回模式库，实现跨目标的经验积累。

Result: 在5个公开dLLM和3个基准上，MaskForge平均攻击成功率为79.3%，比最强dLLM基线相对提升17.6%。训练好的模式库无需更新即可迁移到AdvBench，实现88.2%的攻击成功率和67%的相对提升。

Conclusion: MaskForge证明了通过结构化模式库和经验积累，可以有效利用dLLM特有的安全漏洞（如填充能力、非位置依赖的token提交），实现高效且可迁移的黑盒攻击，揭示了dLLM安全防御的新挑战。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) generate text by iteratively denoising partially masked sequences under bidirectional context, exposing a safety surface distinct from autoregressive LLMs. Because mask tokens are native inputs and tokens are committed by confidence rather than position, harmful content can be induced through infilling and outside the monitored prefix. Existing jailbreaks either miss this native infill capability or rely on low-diversity mask-bearing templates applied uniformly across goals, with little structural adaptation or accumulated attack experience. We propose MaskForge, a fully black-box adaptive attack that casts dLLM red-teaming as optimized search over a growing library of structural patterns. MaskForge abstracts successful attempts into reusable schemas, selects goal-compatible patterns with a UCB bandit, and invokes a scorer-guided fallback when the current library fails. Successful attempts are distilled back into the pattern library, enabling experience to accumulate across goals. Across five public dLLMs and three benchmarks, MaskForge achieves an average attack success rate of 79.3%, a 17.6% relative improvement over the strongest competing dLLM baseline. The matured pattern library further transfers to AdvBench without any updates, achieving a 88.2% attack success rate and a 67% relative improvement over the strongest competing baseline.

</details>


### [81] [Need to Know: Contextual-Integrity-Grounded Query Rewriting for Privacy-Conscious LLM Delegation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04067)
*Xinyue Huang,Xiaochun Cao,Wenyuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出基于语境完整性（Contextual Integrity）的隐私保护查询重写框架，构建了首个任务导向的语境完整性基准 DelegateCI-Bench（含3,167个样本），并设计了CI引导的强化学习方法来训练查询重写器，在保留任务关键信息的同时抑制不必要的敏感信息披露，实验表明该方法在隐私-效用权衡上优于基线方法，平均效用提升最高达+10.1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于类型的PII脱敏方法是上下文无关的，可能导致两个问题：过度披露未类型化的敏感上下文，以及过度移除包含答案的文本。因此需要一种能感知任务上下文的隐私保护方法。

Method: 1) 构建DelegateCI-Bench基准，包含3,167个样本，涵盖11个任务和20种任务类型的高质量合成数据、WildChat真实用户查询以及密集敏感信息的医学挑战集；2) 提出CI引导的强化学习框架，将必要和非必要的敏感片段转化为可验证的优化信号；3) 训练查询重写器以保留任务关键信息同时抑制不必要的敏感披露。

Result: 所学习的重写器在隐私-效用权衡上达到最佳，相较于设备端基线方法平均效用提升最高达+10.1。

Conclusion: 基于语境完整性的方法能有效解决隐私保护查询重写中的隐私-效用权衡问题，通过任务必要性判断来决定敏感信息是否应被转发。

Abstract: As LLMs become increasingly woven into everyday workflows, user queries sent to cloud hosted LLMs routinely mix task-essential content with task non-essential sensitive disclosures, yet type based PII redaction is context agnostic and may raise two issues: over disclosing untyped sensitive context and over removing answer bearing spans. We recast privacy preserving query rewriting under Contextual Integrity: a span should be forwarded only if it is necessary for the task. We introduce DelegateCI-Bench, the first task based Contextual Integrity benchmark for privacy-conscious delegation, comprising 3,167 samples that combine high quality synthetic data spanning 11 tasks and 20 task types, WildChat based real user queries, and a medical challenge set with dense sensitive information. Building on this benchmark, we propose a CI-guided reinforcement learning framework that converts essential and non-essential sensitive spans into verifiable optimization signals, and train a query rewriter to preserve task critical information while suppressing unnecessary sensitive disclosure. Experiments show that our learned rewriter achieves the best privacy-utility tradeoff, achieving up to +10.1 average utility over on-device baselines.

</details>


### [82] [Bayesian Membership Privacy for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04069)
*Sinan Yıldırım,Megha Khosla*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 针对图神经网络（GNN）中传统隐私分析忽视结构相关性和训练图采样的问题，本文提出贝叶斯成员隐私（BMP），一种基于贝叶斯假设检验且考虑节点先验和采样概率的形式化定义，并设计了采样感知的审计机制，以提供比全局攻击精度更细粒度的隐私泄露度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN隐私分析直接沿用非图场景的假设，忽略了结构相关性和随机训练图采样带来的影响。特别是节点依赖的先验信息使得仅靠I型和II型错误无法充分刻画最优成员推断测试，亟需一种适配图结构特性的成员隐私定义。

Method: 提出贝叶斯成员隐私（BMP），将成员推断建模为贝叶斯假设检验，将图采样概率纳入攻击者知识，以后验成员概率量化隐私泄露；并设计采样感知的审计机制来估计BMP参数。

Result: 在基准图数据集上的实验表明，BMP能够提供比全局攻击精度更细粒度的隐私洞察，揭示传统方法无法发现的隐私泄露模式。

Conclusion: BMP为GNN节点级成员隐私提供了更精确的建模框架，弥补了现有定义在图结构场景下的不足，采样感知的审计方法可实际用于度量隐私泄露。

Abstract: Existing privacy analyses for Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) largely inherit assumptions from non-graph settings, overlooking structural correlations and stochastic training-graph sampling. In particular, node-dependent priors make type-I and type-II errors alone insufficient to characterize the best membership inference test. To address this, we introduce Bayesian Membership Privacy (BMP), a sampling-aware formulation of node-level membership privacy that incorporates node-dependent priors and treats graph sampling probabilities as part of the adversary's knowledge. BMP casts membership inference as a Bayesian hypothesis test and accordingly quantifies membership privacy in terms of posterior membership probability. We explore theoretical properties of BMP in relation to the existing definitions in the literature. We further propose a practical, sampling-aware auditing mechanism to estimate the parameters of BMP as a measure of node-level privacy leakage in GNNs. We conduct experiments on benchmark graph datasets and show that BMP yields fine-grained privacy insights that are not visible through global attack accuracy alone.

</details>


### [83] [Covert Influence Between Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04071)
*Avidan Shah,Jay Chooi,Jinghua Ou,Shi Feng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了语言模型在消费彼此输出时存在的隐蔽影响风险，即发送者的行为倾向通过人类无法察觉的载体传递给接收者。作者在监督微调、在策略蒸馏和上下文学习三种接口下刻画了这一风险，发现它们在可实现的影响规模上存在差异。通过使用推理时逐样本归因分数，研究者能够选择放大训练时影响力的载体，实现了前人工作无法达到的载荷传递。进一步证明使用自然语言载体实现的隐蔽影响与使用数字载体的研究是不同现象，后者更难被人类检测且跨模型家族的可移植性更差。这些结果表明隐蔽影响的风险面比先前认识的更广泛，研究者将逐点归因评分方法作为调查和缓解该风险的工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语言模型越来越多地消费彼此的输出，隐蔽影响（即发送者的行为倾向通过人类无法察觉的载体传递给接收者）成为日益增长的风险。现有研究对这一风险在不同接口下的表征尚不充分，且缺乏有效的检测和缓解工具。

Method: 在监督微调、在策略蒸馏和上下文学习三种接口下进行隐蔽影响实验，利用推理时逐样本归因分数选择能够放大训练时影响力的载体，并对比自然语言载体与数字载体的隐蔽影响特性。

Result: 三种接口在可实现的影响规模上存在差异；通过推理时归因分数选择载体可以解锁前人工作无法实现的载荷传递；自然语言载体的隐蔽影响与数字载体是不同现象，后者更难被人类检测且跨模型家族的可移植性更差。

Conclusion: 隐蔽影响的风险面比先前认识的更广泛，逐点归因评分方法可作为调查和缓解该风险的有效工具。

Abstract: As language models increasingly consume one another's outputs, covert influence -- a phenomenon where a sender's payload (the behavioral disposition it is conditioned to propagate) transfers to a receiver through carriers undetectable by humans -- becomes a growing risk. We characterize this risk across three interfaces: supervised fine-tuning, on-policy distillation, and in-context learning, and find that they vary in the scale of influence achievable without leaving behind human-visible traces. Using inference-time per-sample attribution scores, we study covert influence across all three interfaces with the ability to select carriers that amplify training-time influence, unlocking payload transfers that prior work could not achieve. We further provide evidence that covert influence with natural-language carriers is a distinct phenomenon from prior studies using number carriers, as the latter is more resistant to human detection and less portable across model families. Together, these results suggest that the risk surface for covert influence is broader than previously recognized, and we study pointwise attribution scoring methods as a tool to investigate and mitigate it.

</details>


### [84] [Caught in the Act(ivation): Toward Pre-Output and Multi-Turn Detection of Credential Exfiltration by LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04141)
*Kargi Chauhan,Pratibha Revankar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM代理在上下文窗口中混合敏感凭证与不可信检索内容时面临的凭证泄露风险，并提出了三种互补防御方法：激活探针检测、蜜令令牌检测和累积信息流追踪。实验表明这些方法在凭证泄露检测上优于纯文本输出过滤器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理常将敏感凭证与不可信检索内容置于同一上下文窗口，这使得间接提示注入能够诱导凭证泄露。现有文本级输出过滤器不足以防御此类攻击，亟需更全面的防御方案。

Method: 提出三种互补防御：(1) 激活探针：在输出token生成前检测凭证访问行为；(2) 蜜令令牌：基于格式特定字符模型构建蜜令，结合分裂共形预测校准检测；(3) 累积信息流追踪：将多轮泄露视为累积信息流问题，跨对话轮次追踪估计的泄露预算。

Result: 在开放权重模型上，激活特征能够高精度区分良性提示与凭证窃取提示（包括在未见过的编码变换下）。在小型合成多轮测试集上，累积核算能检测到逐轮检测器遗漏的攻击。

Conclusion: 凭证泄露防御应结合输出前监控、校准的蜜令检测和时间累积泄漏核算，而非仅依赖文本级输出过滤器。不过结果仍属初步，多轮基准为内部小规模，激活方法需白盒访问，信息估计器提供的是实用信号而非正式上界。

Abstract: LLM agents often place sensitive credentials in the same context window as untrusted retrieved content, creating a direct path for indirect prompt injection to induce credential exfiltration. We study this failure mode through three complementary defenses. First, we ask whether activation probes can detect credential access before output tokens are emitted. Second, we construct honeytokens from format-specific character models and calibrate detection with split conformal prediction. Third, we treat multi-turn exfiltration as a cumulative information-flow problem and track an estimated leakage budget across conversation turns. In controlled experiments on open-weight models, activation features separate benign and credential-seeking prompts with high accuracy, including under held-out encoding transformations. In a small synthetic multi-turn suite, cumulative accounting detects attacks that per-turn detectors miss. These results are preliminary: the multi-turn benchmark is in-house and small, the activation method requires white-box access, and the information estimator provides a practical signal rather than a formal upper bound. Still, the results suggest that credential-exfiltration defenses should combine pre-output monitoring, calibrated canary detection, and temporal leakage accounting rather than relying only on text-level output filters.

</details>


### [85] [MimeLens: Position-Agnostic Content-Type Detection for Binary Fragments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04171)
*Michael J. Bommarito*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MimeLens是一系列基于BERT的小型编码器，通过对文件任意偏移处的二进制内容进行预训练，实现了无需文件头或固定大小的MIME类型分类。在完整文件头部数据上，其top-1准确率比Magika v1.1高10.7个百分点；在无法访问文件头的场景（如UDP包、磁盘块）中仍能分类，准确率是libmagic和Magika的两倍以上，但单样本CPU延迟高约1-2个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文件类型分类系统（如Magika）假设能够访问文件头部已知偏移处的完整内容，导致在恶意软件分类、取证雕刻、数据包检测和存储索引等实际场景中，面对单个数据包载荷、无头雕刻片段、随机磁盘块或分块上传等输入时失效。

Method: 提出MimeLens，采用小型BERT风格编码器，在文件内均匀随机偏移处对二进制内容进行预训练，不依赖文件头位置信息。支持标准上下文和短上下文两种变体，输入可以是来自文件任意位置的字节块，无需文件头和固定大小，输出libmagic定义的125种MIME标签之一。

Result: 在干净的完整文件头部数据上，MimeLens的top-1准确率比Magika v1.1高10.7个百分点。在中流单个UDP数据包和随机中文件磁盘块场景中，MimeLens仍能分类，且准确率是libmagic和Magika的两倍以上。代价是CPU上单样本延迟比Magika慢约1-2个数量级，但在消费级GPU或批量处理时性能相当。

Conclusion: MimeLens证明了不依赖文件头位置的文件类型分类是可行的，为无头/碎片化场景下的文件类型识别提供了有效方案，所有训练好的检查点已在Hugging Face上开源。

Abstract: File-type classification underlies many workflows like malware triage, forensic carving, packet inspection, and storage indexing. Learned systems such as Google's Magika assume whole-file access at a known offset, so they break on the inputs many of these tasks actually produce, like a single packet payload, a header-less carved fragment, a random disk block, or a chunked upload. We introduce MimeLens, a family of small BERT-style encoders pretrained on binary content from windows sampled at a uniformly random offset within each file, with no privileged head-of-file position, in standard- and short-context variants. A byte chunk goes in from anywhere in a file, no header needed and no fixed size; out comes one of libmagic's 125 MIME labels. On the clean head of complete files, MimeLens beats Magika v1.1 by +10.7 pp top-1 on libmagic-labeled data, and it keeps classifying where Magika cannot: from a single mid-stream UDP packet, and more than twice as accurately as libmagic and Magika on random mid-file disk blocks. The cost is latency: MimeLens runs roughly one to two orders of magnitude slower per sample on CPU than Magika, though it matches on consumer GPUs or in batch. All trained checkpoints are released on Hugging Face (mjbommar/mimelens-001-*).

</details>


### [86] [Notarized Agents: Receiver-Attested Confidential Receipts for AI Agent Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04193)
*Juan Figuera*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 当前AI智能体可观测性存在结构缺陷：记录日志的实体与被记录的实体相同。本文提出Sello协议，通过将信任边界反转——由接收智能体调用的服务使用自身密钥签署收据、加密并发布到透明日志——使智能体所有者无需信任智能体即可重建防篡改轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI智能体可观测性存在结构性缺陷：活动日志的生成者与执行者同一，恶意或被入侵的智能体可以省略、篡改或伪造自身轨迹，而运营商无法独立检测篡改行为。

Method: 提出Sello协议，包含四个核心属性：接收端签名(P1)、使用HPKE加密收据至绑定授权令牌的所有者公钥(P2)、发布至见证者协同签名Merkle日志(P3)、所有者通过令牌引用发现(P4)。协议通过反转信任边界，让服务端而非智能体本身生成可验证收据。

Result: 分析了在攻击者控制智能体及其运营商场景下的安全性，提供了密码学操作的微基准测试，并与Signet、AgentROA、Agent Passport System、draft-farley-acta、SCITT等协议进行了对比定位。

Conclusion: Sello协议通过接收端签名和透明日志机制，实现了不依赖智能体或其运营商的可审计可观测性。同时指出了抑制攻击、服务共谋和采用激励问题等已知局限性。

Abstract: Current AI agent observability is structurally compromised: the entity producing the activity log is the same entity whose activity is being logged. A compromised or buggy agent can omit, alter, or fabricate its own traces, and the operator running the agent has no independent way to detect tampering. We propose a class of protocols that resolves this by inverting the trust boundary: the service that receives an agent's call signs a receipt of what it observed using its own key, encrypts the receipt to the agent's owner, and publishes it to a public transparency log. The owner reconstructs a tamper-evident trail without trusting the agent or its operator. We instantiate the class as Sello, a protocol combining four properties absent in any current system: (P1) receiver-side signing, (P2) HPKE encryption to an owner public key bound to the authorization token via JWS, (P3) publication to a witness-cosigned Merkle log, and (P4) owner-side discovery by token reference. We describe the protocol, analyze its security under an adversary that controls the agent and its operator, present microbenchmarks of the cryptographic operations, and situate Sello among adjacent receipt-protocol work (Signet, AgentROA, Agent Passport System, draft-farley-acta, SCITT). We discuss known limitations including the suppression attack, service collusion, and the adoption-incentive problem.

</details>


### [87] [Long-Term and Short-Term Transistor Aging in Deep Neural Networks: Impact and Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04266)
*Alireza Sarmadi,Virinchi Roy Surabhi,Prashanth Krishnamurthy,Hussam Amrouch,Ramesh Karri,Farshad Khorrami*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本章详细讨论了长期和短期晶体管老化对DNN推理精度的影响，并提出了一种老化感知重训练方法，能在使用激进（小于所需）保护带的情况下生成更鲁棒的DNN，从而缓解老化导致的精度下降。此外，还简要讨论了将短期老化作为硬件木马检测的激励机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DNN在IC硬件实现中受晶体管老化影响导致推理精度下降，而传统添加大保护带的方法会损失性能，因此需要在不牺牲性能的前提下提升DNN对老化效应的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出老化感知重训练（aging-aware retraining）方法，在训练过程中纳入老化效应建模，使得DNN能够在使用更小保护带的情况下仍维持较高推理精度。

Result: 采用老化感知重训练的DNN在使用激进保护带时，其推理精度显著优于未经过该训练的DNN，有效缓解了老化导致的精度退化。

Conclusion: 老化感知重训练是一种有效的DNN可靠性提升策略；此外，短期老化可被用作IC硬件木马检测的激励手段。

Abstract: Deep neural networks (DNNs) are used in a variety of real-world applications including, for example, image classification and speech recognition. The inference accuracy of DNN implemented on hardware in integrated circuits (ICs) degrades under phenomena such as transistor aging. Aging slows down the switching speed of transistors, resulting in system-level timing violations due to unsustainable clocks. To maintain reliability for the entire projected lifetime, designers add guardbands to prevent timing violations; however, adding large timing guardbands causes losses in performance (speed or throughput). This chapter provides a detailed discussion of the effects of long-term and short-term transistor aging on DNN inference accuracy. Furthermore, to mitigate aging effects on DNN's accuracy and keep them at bay, a methodology for aging-aware retraining is presented in order to generate a resilient DNN even when aggressive (i.e., smaller than required) guardbands are used. This improves the inference accuracy of the DNNs even in the presence of aging-induced degradation. These effects are discussed in this chapter along with mitigation strategies on a hardware implementation of a DNN for image classification on an off-the-shelf image dataset. The application of short-term aging as an excitation mechanism for the detection of hardware Trojans in integrated circuits is also briefly discussed.

</details>


### [88] [Formal verification of the S-two AIR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04311)
*Jeremy Avigad,Anat Ganor,Lior Goldberg,David Levit,Ohad Nir,Yoav Seginer,Alon Titelman*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文使用Lean 4证明助手验证了StarkWare的S-two证明系统中AIR编码的可靠性，即AIR的可满足性能够正确蕴含Cairo程序运行完成的计算声明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确保StarkWare的S-two证明系统中AIR编码的可靠性，即证明AIR的可满足性确实能够正确编码Cairo程序的语义，从而保证基于AIR的密码学证明系统的正确性基础。

Method: 使用Lean 4证明助手对AIR编码进行形式化验证，建立AIR的可满足性与Cairo虚拟机语言程序执行完成这一计算声明之间的逻辑蕴涵关系。

Result: 成功在Lean 4中完成了对AIR编码可靠性的形式化验证，确认了AIR的可满足性确实蕴含对应的计算声明。

Conclusion: 该验证工作为circle STARK密码学证明系统的正确性提供了坚实的逻辑基础，增强了StarkWare证明系统的可信度。

Abstract: StarkWare's S-two prover provides an efficient means for establishing, on blockchain, that a program written in the Cairo virtual machine language runs to completion. The latter claim is encoded by an algebraic intermediate representation (AIR) that captures the semantics of the Cairo language. The AIR asserts the existence of tables of values from a finite field satisfying certain algebraic constraints. A cryptographic interactive proof system, circle STARK, provides an efficiently-checked certificate that the AIR is satisfied. We describe our verification, using the Lean 4 proof assistant, that the AIR encoding is sound, which is to say, the satisfiability of the AIR implies the computational claim.

</details>


### [89] [Toward a Generalized Defense Across Sparse, Continuous, and Structured Parameter Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04317)
*Bin Duan,Zeyu Bai,Guowei Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ParDef提出了一种针对深度神经网络参数攻击的通用防御方法，通过密钥通道重参数化、QC-LDPC量化和自适应鲁棒推理三种技术结合，在不重训练的情况下有效防御多种未知参数攻击，在多个数据集和模型上验证了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在异构且部分不可信环境中部署时（云存储、CI/CD流水线、容器化服务、边缘执行平台），模型参数面临多种完整性风险。现有防御方法要么需要重训练、要么精度严重下降、要么仅针对特定攻击类型，而在实际部署场景中参数攻击的形式往往不可预测，因此需要一种通用的防御方案。

Method: ParDef整合了三种关键技术：1) 密钥通道重参数化（Keyed Channel Reparameterization）：模糊敏感参数方向；2) QC-LDPC量化（QC-LDPC Quantization）：嵌入冗余并支持纠错；3) 自适应鲁棒推理（Adaptive Robust Inference）：在不确定性下稳定预测。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和Tiny-ImageNet上使用ResNet和VGG模型的评估表明，ParDef能够一致地降低不同参数攻击的成功率，同时保持较高的模型性能，且部署开销适中。

Conclusion: ParDef是一种实用且通用的DNN部署防御方法，能够有效应对多样化的参数攻击威胁。

Abstract: Deep neural networks are increasingly deployed across heterogeneous and partially untrusted environments, where models are distributed through cloud storage, CI/CD pipelines, containerized services, and edge execution platforms. This broad deployment landscape exposes model parameters to various integrity risks. Unlike input-space adversarial attacks, parameter attacks directly tamper with the model's internal parameters and persist across all subsequent inferences. Existing defenses either require retraining, incur significant accuracy degradation, or are limited to specific attack classes. However, in real-world deployment scenarios, the forms of parameter attacks are often unpredictable. To address this challenge, we present ParDef, a generalized defense for deep neural networks against diverse types of parameter attacks. ParDef integrates keyed channel reparameterization, which obscures sensitive parameter directions, QC-LDPC quantization, which embeds redundancy and supports error correction, and adaptive robust inference, which stabilizes predictions under uncertainty. Our evaluation on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and Tiny-ImageNet using ResNet and VGG models demonstrates that ParDef consistently reduces attack success rates across different parameter attacks while maintaining high model performance and incurring only moderate deployment overhead. These results highlight that ParDef is a practical and generalized defense for DNN deployments.

</details>


### [90] [From Untrusted Input to Trusted Memory: A Systematic Study of Memory Poisoning Attacks in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04329)
*Pritam Dash,Tongyu Ge,Aditi Jain,Tanmay Shah,Zhiwei Shang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了基于LLM的AI智能体中的记忆投毒问题，识别了四种记忆写入通道和九种结构性漏洞，提出了六类记忆投毒攻击的分类体系，并构建了MPBench基准测试，发现更激进记忆读写策略的智能体更易被利用，现有提示注入防御无法覆盖记忆投毒攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体的持久性记忆虽然能积累知识提升性能，但也引入了记忆投毒的风险——单次对抗性记忆写入就能对智能体行为产生长期影响。目前缺乏对这一问题的系统性研究。

Method: 通过识别四种记忆写入通道和九种结构性漏洞（涉及模型能力、系统提示设计、智能体系统架构），建立六类记忆投毒攻击的分类体系，并设计MPBench基准测试来评估攻击效果。

Result: 发现设计上更积极进行记忆读写操作的智能体更容易被利用；现有提示注入防御方法无法有效防御记忆投毒攻击。

Conclusion: 本研究为理解和防御针对AI智能体的记忆投毒攻击提供了基础性框架和方法论支持。

Abstract: Memory is a core component of AI agents, enabling them to accumulate knowledge across interactions and improve performance. However, persistent memory introduces the risk of memory poisoning, where a single adversarial memory write can exert long-term influence over agent behavior. We present a systematic study of memory poisoning in LLM-based agents. We identify four memory write channels and nine structural vulnerabilities in model capabilities, system prompt design, and agent system architecture that make these channels exploitable. Based on these vulnerabilities, we develop a taxonomy of six classes of memory poisoning attacks. Furthermore, we design MPBench -- a benchmark for evaluating memory poisoning attacks, and show that agents designed to write and retrieve memory more aggressively are more exploitable. We also show that existing prompt injection defenses fail to cover memory poisoning attacks. Our findings provide a foundation for understanding and mitigating memory poisoning attacks against AI agents.

</details>


### [91] [TITAN-FedAnil+: Trust-Based Adaptive Blockchain Federated Learning for Resource-Constrained Intelligent Enterprises](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04388)
*Muhammad Hadi,Muhammad Jahangir,Talha Shafique,Muhammad Khuram Shahzad*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出TITAN-FedAnil+框架，一种基于信任的自适应区块链联邦学习系统，通过亲和传播自适应聚类聚合、GPU加速向量化和有符号状态跳跃机制，在资源受限的企业边缘设备上实现81%的内存节省，同时提升鲁棒性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在非独立同分布数据异构性和去中心化安全威胁方面面临挑战，尤其在资源受限的企业环境中，现有方法难以兼顾隐私保护、鲁棒性和计算效率。

Method: 提出TITAN-FedAnil+框架，包含三个核心组件：1）基于亲和传播的自适应聚类聚合，无需先验知识即可识别和过滤恶意更新；2）GPU加速向量化提升计算效率；3）有符号状态跳跃机制实现轻量级区块链重同步。

Result: 在50轮通信中，相较于基线框架，在8GB约束边缘设备上实现高达81%的内存开销节省，显著提升了鲁棒性、可扩展性和资源效率。

Conclusion: TITAN-FedAnil+有效提升了智能企业环境中安全联邦学习部署的鲁棒性、可扩展性和资源效率，适用于资源受限的边缘场景。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) has emerged as an effective paradigm for collaborative intelligence while preserving data privacy. However, data heterogeneity arising from non-IID distributions and decentralized security threats remain significant challenges, particularly in resource-constrained enterprise environments. This paper presents TITAN-FedAnil+, a Trust-Based Adaptive Network for blockchain-enabled federated learning in intelligent enterprises. The proposed framework introduces affinity propagation-based adaptive clustered aggregation to identify and filter malicious updates without requiring prior knowledge of the number of attackers. In addition, GPU-accelerated vectorization is employed to improve computational efficiency, while a signed state jump mechanism enables lightweight blockchain resynchronization. Experimental results demonstrate substantial reductions in memory overhead, achieving up to 81% savings across 50 communication rounds on constrained 8 GB edge devices compared with the baseline framework. The results indicate that TITAN-FedAnil+ effectively improves robustness, scalability, and resource efficiency for secure federated learning deployments in intelligent enterprise environments.

</details>


### [92] [Pepper: High-bandwidth and Scalable Anonymous Broadcast with Cryptographic Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04411)
*Chenghao Li,Haoyuan Wang,Xianghang Mi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Pepper 是一种高带宽匿名广播协议，基于双服务器 DC-net 架构，通过自包含的匿名注册协议、批量消息支持和轻量级访问控制，实现对全局对手的发送者匿名性，比现有方案提升 1.2-20 倍的有效消息速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有匿名广播系统存在两个主要问题：需要依赖外部拨号服务来协调参与者，以及每个发送者每轮只能发送一条消息，导致大数据传输场景下吞吐量受限。Pepper 旨在消除这些瓶颈。

Method: Pepper 构建在双服务器 DC-net 架构上，包含三项核心创新：(1) 使用可验证分布式点函数 (VDPF) 实现自包含的匿名注册子协议，无需外部服务；(2) 利用分布式多点函数 (DMPF) 支持批量消息处理，单个审计即可发送多条消息；(3) 基于秘密共享证明的轻量级访问控制机制。

Result: 实现结果表明：注册审计达到毫秒级；可高效扩展至数千个通道；有效消息速率比现有最先进方案高 1.2-20 倍。

Conclusion: Pepper 是一种实用、可部署的高带宽匿名广播协议，天然兼容与 Tor 和联邦社交网络的协同部署。

Abstract: We present Pepper, a high-bandwidth anonymous broadcast protocol that provides cryptographic sender anonymity against global adversaries. Pepper builds on a two-server DC-net architecture but introduces three key innovations: a self-contained anonymous registration subprotocol using verifiable distributed point functions, support for batch messaging via distributed multi-point functions, and a lightweight access control mechanism based on secret-shared proofs. Unlike prior systems, Pepper eliminates the need for external dialing services and allows each broadcaster to send multiple messages per epoch with a single audit, significantly improving throughput for large data transfers. Our implementation demonstrates that Pepper achieves millisecond-level registration audits, scales efficiently to thousands of channels, and delivers 1.2--20$\times$ higher effective messaging rates than state-of-the-art alternatives. Furthermore, Pepper is designed for practical deployment, with natural compatibility for co-deployment alongside Tor and federated social networks.

</details>


### [93] [What If Prompt Injection Never Left? Exploring Cross-Session Stored Prompt Injection in Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04425)
*Yuanbo Xie,Tianyun Liu,Yingjie Zhang,Suchen Liu,Yulin Li,Liya Su,Tingwen Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出跨会话持久化提示注入攻击，将提示注入从单会话的瞬时模型级威胁转变为跨会话的系统级持久化漏洞，并建立了形式化定义、分类体系、基准测试和沙箱工具包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示注入研究主要关注单会话内的模型级威胁，忽略了智能体系统中跨会话持久化系统状态所带来的系统性风险。

Method: 借鉴Web系统中存储型跨站脚本攻击的思想，形式化定义跨会话存储型提示注入，建立攻击持久化与影响的分类体系，并开发基准测试和沙箱工具包进行定量分析。

Result: 持久化将提示注入从瞬时的模型级威胁转变为嵌入智能体执行状态中的长期系统级漏洞，不同模型、攻击目标和持久化通道下的攻击成功率存在差异。

Conclusion: 智能体系统的持久化特性从根本上扩大了攻击面，需要社区系统性地研究并缓解由此产生的系统级风险。

Abstract: Modern agentic systems transform LLMs from session-bounded assistants into stateful systems that persist and evolve shared world state across sessions through memories, filesystems, tools, and other long-lived contextual artifacts. This shift fundamentally expands the attack surface of prompt injection. However, prior works on prompt injection have largely focused on model-level threats within a single session, overlooking how cross-session persistent system state fundamentally changes the system-level risk of agentic systems. Inspired by stored cross-site scripting in web systems, we introduce cross-session stored prompt injection, where a successful injection can persist within agentic system state and silently influence future executions long after the original attacker interaction has ended. To systematically study this threat, we formalize stored prompt injection and develop a taxonomy of how adversarial content persists and affects agentic systems across sessions. We further develop a benchmark and sandbox toolkit to evaluate the risks of stored prompt injection, enabling quantitative analysis of attack success across different models, attack goals, and persistence channels. Our findings highlight that persistence transforms prompt injection from an ephemeral model-level threat into a long-lived system-level vulnerability embedded within agent execution state. We hope this work draws broader attention to this emerging threat and motivates the community to systematically study and mitigate system risks arising from persistence in agentic systems.

</details>


### [94] [What Can Verifiable Decapsulation Tests Certify? Pass Bounds and Fault-Recognition Limits for FO-Based KEMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04443)
*José Luis Delgado Jiménez*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究基于确认码增强的KEM变体在黑盒测试下的安全性，提出了一个包含列表命中事件与新鲜密钥缺陷的综合安全界，并通过ML-KEM和HQC案例验证了理论覆盖范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Fujisaki-Okamoto解封装黑盒测试仅能观测到采样执行，而重新加密计算仅通过最终密钥派生的可见值间接暴露。为了更精确地刻画黑盒测试下的安全性界限，需要建立确认码增强KEM变体的形式化模型。

Method: 构建了诚实参考封装框架（固定隐藏最终密钥点⟨good,B,W⟩，W为确认见证），定义了q-局部化被测系统的接受界（包含诚实正确性误差、对抗别名、最终密钥新鲜度缺陷、局部后缀列表Q_G(B)命中及2^{-κ}项）。通过任意W预测器的一查询构造匹配该界（至多差一个新鲜密钥重合项），认为列表命中事件是黑盒测试的关键障碍。

Result: 列表命中项可通过cUP忠实框架证书（以q损失转移源确认码不可预测性）或平均条件最小熵界（针对短诊断码和截断尾码的RawEnt与TailEnt假设）界定。同一模型证明了非认证声明的依赖锥下界。当诚实支持框架的黑盒观测经确认可观测最终密钥目标分解时，支持活动锥外的每一操作都存在耦合擦除实现（具有相同转录分布）；在包含该擦除的任意实现类上，执行认证器的声音性与完备性错误满足α+β≥1。

Conclusion: ML-KEM和HQC案例研究区分了定理覆盖的正向行、有限目录伪品行和携带锥非活动证书的非认证行。标准KEM线的安全性由所引源分析提供的构造级安全性保证。

Abstract: Black-box tests for Fujisaki-Okamoto decapsulation observe the sampled execution seen by the harness, whereas the reencryption computation itself is visible only through the values that reach final key derivation. We study confirmation-code-augmented KEM variants under an honest-reference harness in which the reference encapsulation fixes a hidden final-key point $\langle good,B,W\rangle$, with $W$ the confirmation witness. For a $q$-localized system under test, acceptance is bounded by honest correctness error, adversarial aliasing, final-key freshness defects, a hit on the localized suffix list $Q_G(B)$, and $2^{-κ}$. A one-query construction from any predictor of $W$ matches this bound up to the fresh-key coincidence term, so the list-hit event is the black-box obstruction measured by the harness.
  The list-hit term is bounded either by a cUP-faithful harness certificate, which transfers source confirmation-code unpredictability with a $q$-loss, or by an average conditional min-entropy bound, with separate RawEnt and TailEnt hypotheses for short diagnostic and truncation-tail codes. The same model proves a dependency-cone lower bound for non-certification claims. When the black-box observation of an honest-support harness factors through the confirmation-observable final-key target, every operation outside the support-active cone has a coupled erasure implementation with the same transcript distribution; over any implementation class containing that erasure, soundness and completeness errors of an execution certifier satisfy $α+β\ge 1$. The ML-KEM and HQC case studies distinguish theorem-covered positive rows, finite-catalog artifact rows, and non-certification rows that carry a cone-inactivity certificate. The security of the standard KEM lines is the construction-level security supplied by the cited source analyses.

</details>


### [95] [Token Rankings are Unforgeable Language Model Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04459)
*Matthew Finlayson,Andreas Grivas,Xiang Ren,Swabha Swayamdipta*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究发现语言模型的token排名（而非logit值）可作为模型唯一且不可伪造的签名，同时揭示了排名信息与模型参数泄露之间的安全权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在更严格的API限制下（仅暴露token排名而非概率值），排名信息是否仍能作为模型签名，并研究其安全属性及与参数泄露的关系。

Method: 理论分析可行排名集合的唯一性，证明寻找具有相同可行排名集合的模型是NP难问题（多项式不可伪造性），并分析通过排名近似窃取模型最终层参数的可行性。

Result: 每个模型对足够大的k有唯一的可行top-k排名集合；排名签名是首个已知的多项式不可伪造签名；通过排名可近似窃取最终层参数，但近似程度不足以伪造签名；存在k的阈值区间使得API可展示不可伪造签名同时防止参数泄露。

Conclusion: token排名可作为安全且不可伪造的模型身份签名，API可通过合理设置top-k阈值在提供签名认证与保护模型参数之间取得平衡。

Abstract: Language model parameters are known to impose unique (to each model) geometric constraints on their logit outputs, which serves as a signature that identifies the model, but also leaks the model's final layer parameters when an API distributes logits. We investigate more restrictive APIs that expose token rankings (i.e., their ordering by probability, but not the probability values) and find that rankings also constitute a signature: every model has a unique set of feasible top-$k$ rankings for sufficiently large $k$. Furthermore, the ranking signature is the first known (polynomially) unforgeable signature, since finding a model with the same set of feasible rankings is NP-hard. On the security front, we find that token rankings are already sufficient to approximately steal the final layer of the model, similar to logits, though the approximation is too coarse to forge the signature, and can be effectively countered by restricting the API to top-$k$ tokens with sufficiently small $k$. Since the top-$k$ required to present the model signature is generally smaller than the $k$ required to prevent stealing, it is possible for an API to present an unforgeable signature without leaking model parameters.

</details>


### [96] [CyberGym-E2E: Scalable Real-World Benchmark for AI Agents' End-to-End Cybersecurity Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04460)
*Tianneng Shi,Robin Rheem,Dongwei Jiang,Mona Wang,Francisco De La Riega,Zhun Wang,Jingzhi Jiang,Alexander Cheung,Sean Tai,Jonah Cha,Jianhong Tu,Gabriel Han,Chenguang Wang,Jingxuan He,Wenbo Guo,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了CyberGym-E2E，一个大规模、真实的端到端网络安全基准测试，用于全面评估AI智能体在漏洞发现、PoC生成和补丁生成全生命周期中的能力。该基准包含来自139个不同开源项目的920个真实世界漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网络安全评估在规模或范围上有限，未能捕捉真实软件漏洞发现和修复的端到端生命周期，因此需要更全面的评估框架。

Method: 构建了一个自动化、智能体增强的流水线，将开源漏洞数据转化为真实的评估环境，形成包含920个真实漏洞、覆盖139个开源项目的基准测试集。

Result: 建立了包含920个真实漏洞、覆盖139个开源项目的大规模端到端网络安全基准测试。

Conclusion: CyberGym-E2E通过在规模、真实性和全生命周期覆盖上的改进，为评估AI在网络安全领域的端到端能力提供了更全面的基准。

Abstract: AI has the potential to transform cybersecurity by enabling systems that can autonomously detect, analyze, and remediate software vulnerabilities. However, existing cybersecurity evaluations of AI systems are limited in scale or scope, and fail to capture the end-to-end lifecycle of real-world software vulnerability discovery and remediation. To address this gap, we propose CyberGym-E2E, a large-scale and realistic end-to-end cybersecurity benchmark that comprehensively evaluates AI agents' abilities across the full lifecycle of vulnerability discovery, PoC generation, and patch generation. CyberGym-E2E is comprehensive and scalable, as we build an automated, agent-enhanced pipeline for transforming open-source vulnerability data into realistic evaluation environments. Currently, the benchmark consists of 920 real-world vulnerabilities across 139 different open-source projects.

</details>


### [97] [Global Sketch-Based Watermarking for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04486)
*Daniel Zhao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 针对掩码扩散语言模型提出一种基于全局草图表示的文本水印方法，利用生成过程中可处理的加性统计量实现顺序无关的检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水印方法主要针对自回归语言模型设计，依赖局部上下文扰动下一个token分布。扩散语言模型在生成时联合采样多个未确定位置，需要能够利用全局统计量的水印方案。

Method: 提出一种用于掩码扩散语言模型的水印方法，控制文本的向量化草图表示（全局统计量），将检测与生成时的局部上下文解耦，实现顺序无关的统计量和水印规则。

Result: 分析了方法的失真性、可靠性和鲁棒性属性。

Conclusion: 草图公式化方法相比上下文相关的水印方案，避免了简单的token偏差，实现了顺序无关的检测。

Abstract: Watermarking methods for language models have been studied extensively in the autoregressive setting, where tokens are generated sequentially. These works largely focus on local-context schemes that perturb the next token's distribution as a function of its preceding tokens. In diffusion language models, distributions over many unresolved positions are jointly sampled, allowing additive statistics of the entire sequence to be tractable during generation. We propose a watermark for masked diffusion language models that controls a global, vector-valued sketch representation of the text. Compared to context-dependent watermarking, the sketch formulation decouples detection from the local contexts seen during generation, resulting in an order-agnostic statistic and a watermarking rule which does not manifest as a simple token bias. We analyze the distortion, soundness, and robustness properties of the method.

</details>


### [98] [PS-UIE: Privilege-Separated Integrity Enforcement for User-Space Executable Objects in Confidential VMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04549)
*Jingkai Mao,Xiaolin Chang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PS-UIE，一种基于特权分离架构的用户空间可执行对象完整性强制方法，用于在AMD SEV-SNP机密虚拟机中确保从启动到运行时的完整性，并通过策略管理、运行时强制和证据导出机制实现可验证的运行证据生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作未能完全解决机密虚拟机内文件支持的用户空间可执行对象（如主程序、解释器、动态链接库）在运行过程中动态加载或映射时的完整性保证问题。

Method: 设计特权分离架构，将完整性度量和强制的权限从被测目标中分离到更高特权保护域；在此基础上实现策略生命周期管理、执行时完整性强制和证据导出与验证三种机制。

Result: 在AMD SEV-SNP平台上的安全分析与性能评估表明，PS-UIE能在覆盖的执行权限授予路径上强制用户空间可执行对象的完整性，提供可验证的运行证据，且开销可接受。

Conclusion: PS-UIE有效补充了现有CVM完整性保护方案在动态加载用户空间代码方面的不足，为云租户提供了更全面的运行时信任保证。

Abstract: Confidential Virtual Machines (CVMs), such as AMD SEV-SNP, enable cloud tenants to run security-sensitive workloads, but tenants can rely on the execution of these workloads only when they can trust the CVM. This trust requires continuous integrity assurance from CVM launch to the current runtime state, including initial trust establishment at launch and subsequent runtime integrity assurance. Existing works help establish launch-time trust and protect parts of runtime integrity, but they do not fully address the integrity of file-backed user-space executable objects, such as main executables, program interpreters, and dynamically loaded shared objects, that may be loaded or mapped dynamically during execution inside CVMs. In this paper, we propose Privilege-Separated User-space Integrity Enforcement (PS-UIE), an approach for enforcing the integrity of user-space executable objects inside AMD SEV-SNP-based CVMs. PS-UIE consists of a privilege-separated architecture and three mechanisms. The architecture separates the authority for integrity measurement and enforcement from the measured targets by placing it in a higher-privileged protected domain. Built on this architecture, PS-UIE provides policy lifecycle management, execution-time integrity enforcement, and evidence export and verification mechanisms. It enables policy-controlled integrity measurement and enforcement for user-space executable objects and generates verifiable runtime evidence. We implement PS-UIE on an AMD SEV-SNP platform. The security analysis and performance evaluation show that PS-UIE enforces the integrity of user-space executable objects on the covered execute-permission grant paths and provides verifiable runtime evidence while incurring acceptable overhead.

</details>


### [99] [TIBlender: Early-Warning Threat Intelligence from Cross-Platform Social Media Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04580)
*Hiroki Nakano,Takashi Koide,Daiki Chiba*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TIBlender是一个多智能体系统，通过监控X、Reddit、Telegram和Discord四个社交平台，利用角色专业化LLM代理自动生成结构化威胁情报报告，有效捕捉了现有公共情报源未覆盖的早期威胁和IoC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网络威胁信号分散在多个社交平台，缺乏能够完全自动化地将这些碎片化信息整合为可操作威胁情报报告的方法。

Method: 提出TIBlender多智能体系统，监控X、Reddit、Telegram和Discord四个平台，使用角色专业化的LLM代理进行多角度调查、证据链追踪，通过协作式基于证据的分析发现相关IoC。

Result: 在实际部署中，TIBlender在所有四个威胁类别中均早于公共情报源检测到新兴威胁；其大部分IoC未出现在任何评估的公共情报源中；每个平台贡献了其他平台无法获得的独特威胁信息；排除任一平台都会导致特定威胁类别的报告大量损失；全流水线比任何单平台基线提取到更多IoC。

Conclusion: 跨平台社交媒体监控可以作为运营威胁情报管道中有效且可扩展的早期预警层。

Abstract: Cyber threat signals are fragmented across multiple social media platforms, yet no existing approach has fully automated their integration into actionable threat intelligence (TI) reports. We present TIBlender, a multi-agent system that monitors four platforms (X, Reddit, Telegram, and Discord) and produces structured TI reports via role-specialized LLM agents. These agents conduct multi-perspective investigations, tracing chains of evidence to uncover related Indicators of Compromise (IoCs) via collaborative, evidence-backed analysis. In a real-world deployment, TIBlender detected emerging threats across all four threat categories ahead of public feeds, including in-the-wild exploitation ahead of public vulnerability registries; the majority of its IoCs were absent from each evaluated feed. Quantitative evaluation confirms that each platform contributes unique threat information unavailable from the others, and that excluding any single platform results in substantial loss of reports in specific threat categories. Under identical single-platform input conditions, TIBlender's IoC extraction meets or exceeds each baseline; the full pipeline surfaces substantially more IoCs, most of which are absent from any single-platform baseline. These results establish cross-platform social media monitoring as an effective and scalable early-warning layer for operational TI pipelines.

</details>


### [100] [TeleHunt: A Framework and Tool for Efficient Cybercriminal Community Discovery on Telegram](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04657)
*Roy Ricaldi,Victor Asanache,Luca Allodi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出TeleHunt框架，系统评估了不同策略在Telegram上发现网络犯罪社区的效果。通过参考驱动的雪球采样策略，结合消息级分类、上下文过滤和市场细分标签，从开放网络和暗网种子出发，评估了种子来源、指针类型和探索策略对发现效率、可访问性和重新发现率的影响。提供了模块化网络犯罪内容发现流水线、首个系统性的Telegram发现策略比较，以及包含172M+消息、6,022个社区的标注数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Telegram已成为网络犯罪活动的重要平台，但现有研究缺乏对发现策略的系统性评估，无法有效指导如何高效、可靠地定位和访问网络犯罪社区。

Method: 设计并实现TeleHunt框架，采用参考驱动的雪球采样策略，融合消息级分类、上下文过滤和市场细分标签，从开放网络和暗网两种种子源出发，系统评估种子来源、指针类型和探索策略三个维度对发现效率、可访问性和重新发现率的影响。

Result: 构建了包含6,022个社区、超过1.72亿条消息的标注数据集，并首次系统比较了不同Telegram发现策略的效果，实证刻画了市场细分可访问性。

Conclusion: TeleHunt为网络犯罪社区发现提供了模块化、可复用的框架，揭示了种子来源和探索策略对发现效果的系统性影响，为网络犯罪监测研究提供了重要基准和数据集。

Abstract: This paper presents TeleHunt, a framework and tool for evaluating the effectiveness of different strategies to discover cybercriminal communities on Telegram. TeleHunt employs a set of reference-driven snowballing strategies, integrating message-level classification, contextual filtering, and market-segment labeling. Using open- and dark-web seeds, we systematically evaluate how seed source, pointer type, and exploration strategy influence discovery outcomes in three dimensions: efficiency, accessibility, and rediscovery. Our work provides (i) a modular cybercrime content discovery pipeline, (ii) the first systematic comparison of Telegram discovery strategies with an empirical characterization of market-segment accessibility, and (iii) a labeled dataset of over 172 million messages from 6,022 Telegram communities.

</details>


### [101] [SoK: Post-Quantum Cryptography (PQC) Implementation in Software Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04669)
*R. D. N. Shakya,C. P. Wijesiriwardana,S. M. Vidanagamachchi,Nalin A. G. Arachchilage*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过系统化综述（SoK）研究后量子密码（PQC）在软件系统中的实施挑战，从人（Human）、组织（Organisation）、技术（Technology）三个维度进行分析，发现现有研究过度偏重技术层面，而人文与组织因素被忽视。作者提出PQC-HOT模型，将PQC实施视为跨维度的社会技术转型，而非单纯的密码算法替换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要聚焦于PQC算法的性能与安全性，缺乏对影响PQC成功实施的更广泛社会技术因素（如人员技能、组织流程等）的系统理解。研究者希望填补这一空白，揭示不同维度间的相互制约关系。

Method: 采用系统化综述（SoK）方法，从Human（人）、Organisation（组织）、Technology（技术）三个维度系统综合现有PQC实施方法与挑战，分析各维度的研究分布及跨维度关联。

Result: 发现当前知识体系存在显著不平衡：技术解决方案占主导地位，而人力和组织层面的考量严重不足。PQC实施挑战并非孤立存在于单一维度，而是表现为跨越HOT维度的相互关联的社会技术约束，共同影响实施结果。

Conclusion: PQC实施不仅是密码算法替换，更是一场广泛的社会技术转型，需要跨HOT维度的协调策略。提出的PQC-HOT模型为系统决策、规划和组织转型提供了综合框架，并据此提出了未来研究方向和可扩展、可持续的PQC实施设计启示。

Abstract: The transition to Post-Quantum Cryptography (PQC) is essential to protect software systems from emerging quantum-enabled threats. Although standardised PQC algorithms are now available, developers and organisations continue to face significant challenges in integrating them into real-world software systems. While existing studies primarily focus on cryptographic performance and algorithmic security, it provides limited understanding of the broader socio-technological factors that influence successful PQC implementation. This SoK investigates PQC implementation approaches and challenges through the Human, Organisation, and Technology (HOT) dimensions. By systematically synthesising existing approaches across these dimensions, we reveal a notable imbalance in the current body of knowledge, where technological solutions dominate, while human and organisational considerations remain underexplored. Our analysis further shows that PQC implementation challenges are not isolated to individual dimensions; rather, they emerge as interconnected socio-technological constraints that span HOT contexts, collectively shaping implementation outcomes. These findings indicate that PQC implementation extends beyond cryptographic replacement and represents a broader socio-technological transformation requiring coordinated approaches across all HOT dimensions. To address this gap, we propose the PQC-HOT model, a conceptual framework that explains how interactions among HOT dimensions collectively influence PQC implementation in software. The model synthesises the implementation interventions and challenges identified in the SoK into an integrated structure that supports systematic decision-making, planning, and organisational transition strategies. Based on these insights, we outline future research directions and design implications for scalable and sustainable PQC implementation in software systems.

</details>


### [102] [Selection-Aware Diagnostics for Chain-of-Thought Answer Hijacking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04717)
*Jianwei Tai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究链式思维（CoT）答案劫持的受控数值代理，发现CoT包装器可翻转GSM8K和MATH-500的正确答案。通过激活修补定位脆弱点，发现少数样本/谜题劫持在Bonferroni校正后通过K=1定位验证，选择感知的50/50波段验证保留带内-带外差距。Qwen-谜题和Llama3-少数样本/谜题表现稳定，而Qwen-少数样本仍处于探索阶段，谄媚劫持在短修补下呈现时间弥散。BF16全波段扫描确认信号非INT4伪影。固定钩子重跑恢复率分别为47.0%（Qwen-谜题）和39.0%（Llama3-谜题），冻结迁移至MATH-500恢复26.0%。源控制改变机制解释，配对bootstrap显示Llama3-少数样本为内容介导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明链式思维（CoT）推理可能被恶意利用，通过看似良性的推理引导出有害回答。本文旨在系统研究这种"答案劫持"现象的脆弱性，理解在何种条件下可以恢复被劫持的正确回答，以及恢复是否需要来自同一问题的干净源，为防范此类攻击提供理论基础。

Method: 采用受控数值代理模拟CoT答案劫持，在GSM8K和MATH-500数据集上使用CoT包装器翻转答案。使用激活修补（activation patching）技术定位轨迹脆弱点，采用Bonferroni校正进行多重假设检验。设计选择感知的50/50波段验证方法，通过BF16全波段扫描排除量化伪影。实施固定钩子重跑实验测量恢复率，以及冻结迁移至MATH-500的泛化实验。使用配对bootstrap进行有限样本非分离检验。

Result: 三种少数样本/谜题劫持通过Bonferroni校正后的K=1定位验证；50/50波段验证保留带内-带外差距分别+32.6、+45.1、+17.7点；BF16全波段扫描确认信号真实性（n=30，spread 0.33 at K=1，peak layer 20）；固定钩子重跑恢复率Qwen-谜题47.0%，Llama3-谜题39.0%；冻结迁移至MATH-500恢复26.0%；配对bootstrap显示Llama3-少数样本为内容介导（+40.0），而Qwen-少数样本和Llama3-谜题无法分离干净源与随机源。

Conclusion: CoT答案劫持的脆弱性存在可定位的结构化模式，部分场景下恢复不依赖特定干净源，而Llama3-少数样本场景中内容介导起关键作用。研究为理解大语言模型推理安全性和设计防御机制提供了实验基础。

Abstract: We study a controlled numeric proxy for chain-of-thought (CoT) answer hijacking, motivated by attacks in which benign-looking reasoning steers a harmful final answer. CoT wrappers on GSM8K and MATH-500 flip final answers away from gold labels. Rather than treating activation patching as clean-trace restoration, we ask where hijacked trajectories are fragile and whether recovery depends on a same-problem clean source. Across Qwen2.5-7B and Llama3-8B on GSM8K few-shot, puzzle, and sycophant hijacks, three few-shot/puzzle cells pass confirmatory $K{=}1$ localization after Bonferroni correction. A selection-aware 50/50 band validation preserves held-out in-band minus out-of-band gaps of +32.6, +45.1, and +17.7 points for Qwen-puzzle, Llama3-fewshot, and Llama3-puzzle, while exact $\Lstar$ agreement is much less stable. Qwen-fewshot remains exploratory, and sycophant cells are temporal-diffuse under short patches. A BF16 Qwen-puzzle full-band sweep preserves the band signal ($n{=}30$, spread 0.33 at $K{=}1$, peak layer 20), supporting the conclusion that the band is not only an INT4 artifact. Fixed-hook GSM8K reruns preserve recovery in both primary puzzle cells: Qwen-puzzle recovers 47.0\% at $n{=}100$ (47/100; Wilson 95\% CI [37.5\%, 56.7\%]), while Llama3-puzzle recovers 39.0\% at $n{=}100$ (39/100; [30.0\%, 48.8\%]). Frozen transfer to MATH-500 recovers 26.0\% of qualified cases in the largest fixed-transfer run (13/50; Wilson 95\% CI [15.9\%, 39.6\%]). Source controls change the mechanism interpretation. Paired bootstraps give finite-sample non-separation between clean and random sources in Qwen-fewshot (+3.0 points, 95\% CI [-18.2,+27.3]) and Llama3-puzzle at expanded $n{=}60$ (clean--random -8.3 [-21.7,+5.0]), while Llama3-fewshot is content-mediated (+40.0 [+16.7,+60.0]).

</details>


### [103] [Description-Code Inconsistency in Real-world MCP Servers: Measurement, Detection, and Security Implications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04769)
*Yutao Shi,Xiaohan Zhang,Xiangjing Zhang,Xihua Shen,Hui Ouyang,Huming Qiu,Mi Zhang,Min Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了MCP服务器中工具描述与代码实现不一致（DCI）的问题，通过DCIChecker框架对2,214个真实MCP服务器的19,200个描述-代码对进行分析，发现9.93%存在不一致，并提出了缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP协议中LLM依赖工具描述来选择和执行函数，但实际部署中工具描述与其底层实现的一致性缺乏强制验证，可能导致安全漏洞和功能异常。

Method: 提出DCIChecker框架，结合结构感知静态分析与Direct-Reverse-Arbitration提示方法，对工具描述与代码实现进行交叉验证；并基于2,214个真实MCP服务器构建大规模数据集进行测量。

Result: 在19,200个描述-代码对中，9.93%存在功能不一致或未声明副作用等DCI问题，且DCI会导致从操作失败到隐蔽恶意行为的多种风险。

Conclusion: DCI是MCP生态系统中普遍存在的安全问题，需通过语义一致性强制验证等策略来增强代理系统的可靠性。

Abstract: The Model Context Protocol (MCP) has emerged as a critical standard empowering Large Language Models (LLMs) to utilize external tools. In this ecosystem, LLMs rely on natural language descriptions provided by MCP servers to select and execute functions. This interaction implicitly assumes that tool descriptions faithfully reflect their underlying implementations, while this assumption is not mandatorily verified in practice. As a result, MCP deployments may suffer from a problem named Description-Code Inconsistency (DCI), where a tool's description of its capabilities and security boundaries is not consistent with what the code actually does.
  In this paper, we present a comprehensive study of DCI in real-world MCP servers. We formally define the problem and propose a comprehensive taxonomy spanning functionality inconsistencies and undeclared side effects. Guided by this taxonomy, we develop DCIChecker, an automated framework that combines structure-aware static analysis with the Direct-Reverse-Arbitration prompting method to cross-validate tool descriptions against actual code implementations. We apply this framework to a large-scale dataset comprising 19,200 description-code pairs extracted from 2,214 real-world MCP servers. Our measurement reveals that DCI is widespread, with 9.93% of these pairs exhibiting inconsistencies. We further demonstrate that DCI creates a critical defense blind spot, facilitating varied risks from operational failures to stealthy malicious behaviors. Finally, we propose mitigation strategies to enforce semantic consistency and enhance the reliability of the emerging agentic ecosystem.

</details>


### [104] [The Usefulness Gap in Proof-of-Useful-Work: An Empirical Study of Pearl's cuPOW Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04819)
*Abhinaba Basu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Pearl的Layer-1区块链声称通过PoUW协议同时保障网络安全和执行AI推理，但实证测量发现其24 EH/s网络（约320,000个GPU等价物，耗电112 MW）产生了零有用AI计算。矿机发布后GPU租赁价格上涨38%，利用率从57%飙升至94%，排挤了合法研究工作。五项测量维度（网络组成、验证协议、统计检验、盈利能力、计算类型）均表明该系统未能实现有用工作证明的承诺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对首个实际部署的PoUW系统进行系统性实证测量，检验"有用工作证明"能否在真实环境中同时保障网络安全和执行有用计算，量化可验证性与有用性之间的理论张力。

Method: 从五个维度进行测量：(1)分析8,012个工作节点的网络组成；(2)测试验证协议接受随机矩阵的能力；(3)检验统计分布检查的鲁棒性；(4)计算不同GPU层级的挖矿投资回报率；(5)分析挖矿计算的硬件可移植性。

Result: Pearl网络尽管拥有AI推理硬件，但主控矿工软件不含推理代码；验证协议接受随机矩阵；统计检验可被对抗性高斯采样轻易破解；所有GPU层级挖矿均不盈利（ROI -54%至-72%）；挖矿计算为可移植的通用整数运算，无供应商锁定效应。

Conclusion: Pearl的PoUW系统未能产生任何有用AI计算，其验证机制存在根本性缺陷，证实了可验证性与有用性之间的不可调和矛盾，量化了该理论张力在部署系统中的经济后果。

Abstract: Pearl, a Layer-1 blockchain with high-profile AI industry endorsements, markets its Proof-of-Useful-Work (PoUW) protocol as simultaneously securing the network and performing AI inference. We present the first systematic empirical measurement of a deployed PoUW system, finding that Pearl's 24 EH/s network -- representing approximately 320,000 GPU-equivalents consuming an estimated 112 MW -- produces zero useful AI computation. Budget GPU rental prices rose 38% and utilization surged from 57% to 94% following the mining software's public release, displacing legitimate research workloads.
  Our measurements span five dimensions: (1) network composition analysis of 8,012 workers shows all have inference-capable hardware, yet the dominant mining software contains no inference code; (2) the verification protocol accepts random matrices by design, confirmed by 44 pool-accepted shares from our open-source miner across NVIDIA, AMD, CPU, and Apple Silicon hardware; (3) statistical distribution checks are trivially defeated by adversarial Gaussian sampling; (4) mining is unprofitable at current PRL prices ($0.21) across all GPU tiers (-54% to -72% ROI); and (5) the mining computation is commodity integer arithmetic portable to any hardware platform, offering no vendor lock-in. These findings quantify the verifiability-usefulness tension identified theoretically by Leinweber et al., providing concrete measurements of its magnitude and economic consequences in a deployed system.

</details>


### [105] [ODYSSEY: Reestablishing Confidentiality in Confidential Blockchain via Delegated Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04892)
*Ju Yang,Weili Wang,Jianyu Niu,Jianzong Wang,Yinqian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ODYSSEY 是一种基于 TEE 的新型机密区块链系统，通过委托人模型、位置感知并发执行和委托失败处理机制，有效缓解执行推断和执行重放两类攻击，在 FISCO BCOS 上实现 3 节点广域网环境下约 4000 吞吐量和 0.4-0.5 秒延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于 TEE 的机密联盟区块链面临两类安全威胁：执行推断攻击（利用 TEE 的长期侧信道泄露交易信息）和执行重放攻击（利用 TEE 状态连续性泄露历史执行信息），需要一种既能保护机密性又保持高性能的解决方案。

Method: (1) 委托人模型：客户端将交易执行委托给指定的受托人，其他节点仅同步执行结果，缩小攻击面；(2) 位置感知并发执行：优化并发执行效率；(3) 委托失败处理机制：处理委托执行失败的情况。

Result: 在 FISCO BCOS 企业级联盟链平台上实现原型，3 节点广域网环境下达到约 4000 吞吐量，延迟维持在 0.4-0.5 秒。

Conclusion: ODYSSEY 通过创新的委托人模型和配套优化技术，在不牺牲系统性能的前提下有效缓解了 TEE 机密区块链面临的两类核心攻击，为机密集群区块链提供了高效且安全的解决方案。

Abstract: Confidential blockchains leveraging Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) have garnered extensive attention for transaction confidentiality. In this paper, we first taxonomize two classes of attacks against confidential blockchains, i.e., execution-inference and execution-replay attacks, which exploit TEEs' long-lasting side-channel and state-continuity issues to compromise the confidentiality of existing consortium blockchains. Then, we present ODYSSEY, a confidential blockchain that efficiently mitigates these attacks. The core innovations of ODYSSEY are the following: (1) Its delegation model: clients delegate transaction execution to their designated trustees, while other participants synchronize only the execution results, which significantly reduces the attack surface while preserving confidentiality and system performance. (2) Two novel techniques to improve ODYSSEY's efficiency and security: location-aware concurrent execution and delegation failure handler. Finally, we develop a prototype of ODYSSEY on FISCO BCOS, an enterprise-grade consortium blockchain platform. We have conducted various experiments, and our evaluation results show that in a WAN environment with 3 nodes, ODYSSEY can achieve about 4k throughput while keeping latency as low as 0.4-0.5s.

</details>


### [106] [DIST-FL: Enhancing Security for TEE-based Aggregation in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04899)
*Guanlong Wu,Ju Yang,Zhen Huang,Jianyu Niu,Guoxing Chen,Jianzong Wang,Yinqian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出DIST-FL，一种基于多TEE的分布式联邦学习系统，通过操作线性化和可靠服务器输入机制，有效防御状态回滚和I/O操纵攻击，在保证隐私和鲁棒性的同时实现6倍吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TEE辅助的联邦学习协议仍面临服务器端攻击者利用TEE的状态回滚和I/O操纵漏洞操纵客户端选择和重复聚合，破坏系统鲁棒性和隐私性。

Method: 提出DIST-FL分布式系统，由多个TEE保护的服务器构成仅追加分类账本，通过操作线性化防御状态回滚攻击，并整合可靠服务器输入以缓解I/O操纵威胁。

Result: 在广域网设置下的实验表明，DIST-FL能有效抵御所提出的攻击，在匹配单TEE性能的同时，利用TEE计算优势实现6倍于同类方案的吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: DIST-FL通过多TEE分布式架构有效解决了TEE在联邦学习场景下的安全漏洞，为隐私保护和鲁棒聚合提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs)-aided federated learning protocols emerge as promising solutions to counter server-side adversaries and ensure the trustworthiness of the server. In this paper, we dissect existing protocols and demonstrate that server-side adversaries can still manipulate client selection and replay aggregation to compromise system robustness and privacy, by exploiting TEE limitations, i.e., state rollback and I/O manipulation. To this end, we present DIST-FL, a distributed system of servers guarded by multiple TEEs forming an append-only ledger for privacy-preserved, robust FL aggregation. Specifically, DIST-FL ensures operation linearizability to thwart state rollback attacks and incorporates inputs from reliable servers to mitigate I/O manipulation threats. We implement DIST-FL and conduct evaluations in WAN settings. Experimental results demonstrate that DIST-FL can effectively counter the proposed attacks and match the single-TEE's performance while offering a 6x throughput boost over its counterparts, leveraging TEE's computational advantages.

</details>


### [107] [CLIF: Cross-layer LEO-ISL Fingerprinting for Physical and Network Attack Detection in Dense LEO Constellations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04901)
*Varun Kohli,Arijit Bhattacharjee,Samar Shailendra,Biplab Sikdar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种跨层轻量级行为指纹框架，融合物理层与网络层数据，用于检测LEO巨型星座（星链、Kuiper及多运营商联合场景）中的网络层与复合攻击。基于马氏距离的检测器在三个星座上分别达到99.5%、99.4%和94.8%的召回率，误报率低于0.7%，证明了跨层特征融合在高能效约束下的有效性与必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LEO巨型星座（如星链、Kuiper）依赖星间激光链路进行自主网格路由，其链路完整性对商业可用性和国家安全至关重要。然而现有实时安全方法仅聚焦物理层安全，缺乏对网络层及复合攻击的覆盖，且缺乏真实世界数据支撑。

Method: 提出跨层轻量级行为指纹框架，融合星上物理层测量与网络层数据；构建覆盖星链（1,584颗）、Kuiper（1,156颗）及多运营商联合场景（2,740颗）的轨道仿真，注入十种涵盖欺骗、流量操纵和路由颠覆的攻击类型；评估三种无监督单星检测器。

Result: 基于马氏距离的检测器在星链上召回率99.5%，Kuiper上99.4%，多运营商联合星座上94.8%，误报率均低于0.7%。

Conclusion: 跨层特征融合对于LEO星座的全面安全防护是必要的，且在资源受限卫星的严格星上能量预算下，对于大规模网络具有高成本效益。

Abstract: Low-Earth Orbit (LEO) mega-constellations such as Starlink by SpaceX and Kuiper by Amazon rely on optical Inter-Satellite Links (ISLs) for autonomous mesh routing to provide low-latency telecommunication, Internet of Things (IoT), and security services globally. As commercial operators and governments deploy increasingly dense constellations and form multi-operator peering coalitions, ISL integrity becomes critical to both commercial availability and national security. However, there is a lack of real-world data for LEO constellations and existing real-time security approaches focus strictly on physical layer security, leaving blind spots in the coverage of network-layer and composite attacks. In this paper, we present a cross-layer, lightweight behavioral fingerprinting framework that fuses onboard physical-layer measurements with network-layer data to detect anomalies at low computational overhead. We construct an orbital simulation covering the first shells of Starlink (1,584 satellites), Kuiper (1,156 satellites), and a joint multi-operator peering scenario (2,740 satellites), injecting ten attack types that span spoofing, traffic manipulation, and routing subversion at varying severity. We evaluate three unsupervised, per-satellite detectors among which our Mahalanobis-distance-based detector achieves 99.5% recall on Starlink, 99.4% on Kuiper, and 94.8\% on the multi-operator constellation, while maintaining False Positive Rates (FPR) below 0.7%. Our results demonstrate that cross-layer feature fusion is not only necessary for comprehensive security of LEO constellations but highly cost-effective for large-scale networks while fitting into the strict onboard energy budgets of resource-constrained satellites.

</details>


### [108] [TeeDAO: A Decentralized Autonomous Organization for Heterogeneous TEEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04912)
*Pinshen Xu,Wentao Dong,Guoxing Chen,Jianyu Niu,Cong Wang,Yinqian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出TeeDAO，一个三层框架，通过异构TEE、BFT共识、分布式主动秘密共享(DPSS)和安全多方计算(MPC)的协同设计，实现去中心化信任、长期安全保证与统一接口，在61节点集群中KV存储吞吐量提升1.8倍，MPC计算开销低于18%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单TEE方案存在单点攻击风险，而基于异构TEE的分布式信任系统仍面临集中管理和自适应移动 adversary 的威胁，缺乏长期可用性、完整性和机密性保证。

Method: 提出TeeDAO三层框架：将BFT排序的治理机制与异构感知的DPSS和MPC相结合，使基于远程证明的委员会变更一致地反映在秘密恢复、重新共享和计算中，支持Intel SGX、TDX和Hygon CSV等多种TEE。

Result: 集成COBRA的DPSS方案与HotStuff BFT共识协议，在61节点集群中相比现有系统KV存储吞吐量提升1.8倍，实现高效自治管理，MPC任务计算开销低于18%。

Conclusion: TeeDAO通过去中心化信任架构有效缓解了单点攻击和集中管理风险，验证了异构TEE组合在保障长期安全属性方面的可行性与性能优势。

Abstract: Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) have emerged as a critical technology for safeguarding sensitive data and ensuring code integrity in modern computing systems. However, relying on a single TEE implementation makes systems vulnerable to a central point of attack. Building distributed-trust systems leveraging heterogeneous TEEs helps disperse trust but still faces threats from centralized management and adaptive mobile adversaries. To address these challenges, this paper introduces TeeDAO, a novel three-layer framework that automatically organizes multiple heterogeneous TEE instances and provides unified interfaces to support diverse applications, while ensuring long-term guarantees of availability, integrity, and confidentiality. TeeDAO couples BFT-ordered governance with heterogeneity-aware Distributed Proactive Secret Sharing (DPSS) and Secure Multi-Party Computation (MPC) so that attestation-driven committee changes are consistently reflected in secret recovery, resharing, and computation across a dynamic committee of heterogeneous TEEs. We implement a prototype of TeeDAO, integrating COBRA's DPSS scheme with the HotStuff BFT consensus protocol, and adapt it for Intel SGX, TDX, and Hygon CSV. Evaluations demonstrate that TeeDAO achieves up to 1.8x higher key-value store throughput in a large cluster with 61 nodes compared to state-of-the-art systems, efficient autonomous management, and minimal computation overhead (<18%) for multi-party computation tasks.

</details>


### [109] [NLLog: Lightweight, Explainable SOC Anomaly Detection via Log-to-Language Rewriting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04957)
*Samuel Ndichu,Tao Ban,Seiichi Ozawa,Takeshi Takahashi,Daisuke Inoue*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: NLLog是一个轻量级管道，通过将解析后的日志模板确定性重写为WHO-WHAT-SEVERITY句子，结合TF-IDF加权和树集成分类，实现日志异常检测与可解释分析，在HDFS、BGL和AIT数据集上超过基线方法，并保持适合SOC运维的低延迟和高效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统生成的日志虽然支撑安全监控，但其基于僵化模板的格式既阻碍自动化分析，也妨碍人类理解，因此需要一种既能保持可审计性又能提升可解释性的日志表示方法。

Method: 提出NLLog管道：将解析后的日志模板确定性重写为WHO-WHAT-SEVERITY自然语言句子；使用TF-IDF加权对句子池化；采用树集成（随机森林）对会话进行分类；利用TreeSHAP进行后向投影，为分析师提供可回溯的证据。

Result: 在HDFS和BGL数据集上超过两个复现的匹配协议基线；在三个数据集上保持低假阳性率，且硬件延迟适合安全运营中心（SOC）的日常分诊工作。

Conclusion: 回退充分性依赖于语料库；部署前可通过注册时覆盖率检查发现优化需求；可审计的确定性重写结合轻量级稠密编码可为日志异常检测与分诊提供可衡量的表示层。

Abstract: System-generated logs underpin security monitoring, yet their rigid template-based format hinders both automated analysis and human comprehension. We present NLLog (Natural-Language Log), a lightweight pipeline that deterministically rewrites parsed templates into WHO-WHAT-SEVERITY sentences, pools them with term-frequency-inverse-document-frequency weighting, classifies sessions with tree ensembles, and back-projects evidence with TreeSHAP for analyst review. On Hadoop Distributed File System (HDFS) and Blue Gene/L (BGL) corpora, NLLog exceeds two reproduced matched-protocol baselines; across HDFS, BGL, and the AIT Alert Data Set, it sustains low false-positive rates with commodity-hardware latency suitable for security operations center triage. Coverage, sparse-versus-dense, faithfulness, and adversarial ablations show that fallback sufficiency is corpus-dependent, that an enrollment-time coverage check can surface refinement requirements before deployment, and that an auditable deterministic rewrite combined with lightweight dense encoding provides a measurable representation layer for log-anomaly detection and triage.

</details>


### [110] [From Agent Traces to Trust: Evidence Tracing and Execution Provenance in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04990)
*Yiqi Wang,Jiaqi Zhang,Taotao Cai,Zirui Liu,Qingqiang Sun,Zequn Sun,Zhangkai Wu,Mingkai Zhang,Yanming Zhu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了基于大语言模型（LLM）的智能体在执行任务过程中的证据追踪与执行溯源问题，提出了统一溯源视角的概念框架，涵盖溯源来源、单元、关系、粒度、表示形式与信任函数，并梳理了方法方向、基准评测与开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体通过与外部工具、检索系统、记忆模块、环境及其他智能体的交互来解决复杂任务，这种能力增强了自主性，但也使得行为更难验证、调试和审计。仅靠最终答案的准确性无法解释输出如何产生、每个主张由哪些证据支持、工具调用是否合理、记忆如何影响后续决策以及执行失败的根源。证据追踪与执行溯源通过建模检索证据、工具输出、记忆项、环境观察、中间主张、动作与最终答案之间的连接关系来填补这一空白。

Method: 采用系统性综述方法，构建了统一溯源视角的概念框架。提出了涵盖以下维度的分类体系：溯源来源、证据与执行单元、溯源关系、追踪粒度与时机、表示形式、信任函数。梳理了关键方法论方向，包括溯源表示、证据归因、工具使用溯源、运行时护栏、携带溯源的记忆、基于轨迹的可观测性以及故障诊断。同时对现有基准、数据集和评估指标进行了图谱映射。

Result: 建立了统一的溯源视角框架，将检索接地、主张支持、工具使用安全、记忆谱系、可观测性、调试、审计与恢复联系起来。提出了包含多维度要素的溯源分类体系，梳理了七大方法论方向，并评估了现有基准与数据集的溯源相关能力覆盖情况。

Conclusion: 评估应从最终答案正确性转向过程级问责性。开放挑战包括：统一溯源模式、主张级与语义级溯源、溯源感知的安全机制、真实的执行轨迹基准、面向恢复的评估以及隐私感知的审计基础设施。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based agents increasingly solve complex tasks by interacting with external tools, retrieval systems, memory modules, environments, and other agents. These capabilities expand agent autonomy, but also make agent behavior harder to verify, debug, and audit. Final-answer accuracy alone cannot explain how an output was produced, which evidence supported each claim, whether tool calls were justified, how memory influenced later decisions, or where execution failures originated. Evidence tracing and execution provenance address this gap by modeling how retrieved evidence, tool outputs, memory items, environment observations, intermediate claims, actions, and final answers are connected throughout agent execution. This survey provides a systematic review and conceptual framework for evidence tracing and execution provenance in LLM agents. We organize related work around a unified provenance perspective that connects retrieval grounding, claim support, tool-use safety, memory lineage, observability, debugging, audit, and recovery. We introduce a taxonomy covering trace sources, evidence and execution units, provenance relations, tracing granularity and timing, representation forms, and trust functions. We review key methodological directions, including provenance representation, evidence attribution, tool-use provenance, runtime guardrails, provenance-bearing memory, trace-based observability, and failure diagnosis. We also map existing benchmarks, datasets, and evaluation metrics to provenance-related capabilities, and discuss how evaluation can move from final-answer correctness toward process-level accountability. Finally, we outline open challenges, including unified trace schemas, claim-level and semantic provenance, provenance-aware safety mechanisms, realistic execution-trace benchmarks, recovery-oriented evaluation, and privacy-aware audit infrastructure.

</details>


### [111] [SharedRequest: Privacy-Preserving Model-Agnostic Inference for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05004)
*Peihua Mai,Xuanrong Gao,Youlong Ding,Xianglong Du,Wei Liu,Yan Pang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SharedRequest是一个模型无关的隐私保护LLM推理框架，通过在批次级别混合原始提示与噪声变体来保护用户提示隐私，同时分组语义等价指令以分摊推理成本，相比差分隐私基线实现了超过20%的效用提升，并降低了最高5倍的查询成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着ChatGPT等公共LLM的广泛部署，用户提示隐私保护变得日益重要，现有隐私推理方法牺牲了效用或效率，且通常需要模型特定修改，限制了兼容性。

Method: 提出SharedRequest框架，将隐私保护从单个提示级别提升到批次级别，通过将原始提示与噪声变体混合来隐藏敏感信息，同时分组语义等价指令以批量查询分摊推理成本，该方法无需访问模型参数或修改架构，与LLM架构无关。

Result: 相比差分隐私基线实现超过20%的效用提升，共享提示机制相比非批量推理降低最多5倍查询成本。

Conclusion: SharedRequest在保护用户提示隐私的同时，实现了更高的效用和更低的查询成本，且具有模型无关性，兼容性好。

Abstract: With the widespread deployment of public large language models (LLMs) such as ChatGPT, protecting user prompt privacy has become an increasingly critical issue. Existing privacy-preserving inference methods sacrifice either utility or efficiency, and often require model-specific modifications that limit their compatibility. In this paper, we propose SharedRequest, a model-agnostic framework for privacy-preserving LLM inference that reformulates privacy protection at the batch level rather than the individual-prompt level. The key idea is to obscure sensitive information by mixing original prompts with noisy variants, while grouping semantically equivalent instructions to amortize the inference cost over a large batch of queries with minimal impact on LLM response quality. This design is independent of the LLM architecture, requiring no access to model parameters or architectural modification. Empirical results demonstrate that SharedRequest achieves over $20\%$ higher utility compared to prior differential privacy baselines, and its shared-prompt mechanism reduces query cost by up to $5\times$ compared to non-batched inference.

</details>


### [112] [Attention-Augmented LSTMs for Automatic Homophonic Ciphertext Decipherment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05078)
*Micaella Bruton,Meriem Beloucif,Beáta Megyesi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文评估了注意力增强LSTM模型在历史共享密钥设置下对同音替代密码的解密能力。模型仅通过对齐的密文-明文对训练，无需外部语言模型或频率统计，在合成数据上实现了近乎完美的字符级解密准确率，并能用于密钥空间验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同音替代密码通过一对多的映射关系削弱了字母频率模式，使自动解密变得困难。本文旨在探索注意力增强LSTM模型是否能在历史共享密钥设置下学习这种映射关系，为密码分析提供实用工具。

Method: 使用注意力增强的LSTM模型，在共享密钥设置下进行训练。密文来自ChronoFidelius生成的合成数据，涵盖历史英语和瑞典语文本（1500-1899年）。模型仅使用对齐的密文-明文对进行训练，不依赖外部语言模型、频率统计或密钥搜索启发式方法。测试了不同密文长度、世纪、变长编码和模拟转录错误下的性能。

Result: 模型在两种语言和所有历史时期均实现了近乎完美的字符级解密准确率，包括短密文和含噪密文。对于共享池之外的密文，模型可预测地失败，表明其可用于密钥空间验证。

Conclusion: 注意力增强LSTM模型在共享密钥同音替代密码的解密中表现出色，可作为实用工具用于解密和密钥空间验证，特别是当怀疑密钥被重复使用时。

Abstract: Homophonic substitution ciphers replace each plaintext letter with one of several possible ciphertext codes, deliberately weakening letter-frequency patterns and making automated decipherment difficult. This paper evaluates whether an attention-augmented Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) model can learn such mappings in a historically motivated shared-key setting: all ciphertexts draw from the same known homophonic code pool, while individual keys use different consistent subsets of that pool. Using synthetic ciphertexts generated with ChronoFidelius from historical English and Swedish texts dated 1500--1899, we test performance across ciphertext lengths, centuries, variable-length codes, and simulated transcription errors. Models are trained only on aligned ciphertext--plaintext pairs, without external language models, frequency statistics, or key-search heuristics. Results show near-perfect character-level decryption accuracy across both languages and all periods, including short and noisy ciphertexts. The model also fails predictably on ciphertexts outside the shared pool, indicating that it functions as a practical tool for decipherment and key-space verification when key reuse is suspected.

</details>


### [113] [Bernoulli CUSUM and Bayes-Optimal Detection Ceilings for Trust Fraud in Sparse Rating Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05090)
*Talal Ashraf Butt*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文证明了在比特币OTC等评分网络中，由于56%的评分在标准映射下为单值，传统连续观测模型失效。通过推导贝叶斯最优F1检测上限（中位入度为2时低至0.451），发现检测器-模型匹配（而非信息量）决定性能。提出双机制架构（伯努利CUSUM+非对称评分），在8种攻击下优于GaaSTrust（p<0.003），AUC达0.749-0.999。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列信任检测方法依赖连续观测模型，但在真实评分网络（如比特币OTC）中，大量评分映射后呈现单值分布，破坏了参数化检测器的分布假设，导致检测性能严重受限。

Method: 1) 推导基于经验观测参数的逐节点序列检测器的贝叶斯最优F1检测上限；2) 通过互信息分析证明二元模型保留86%信息量；3) 提出双机制架构：伯努利CUSUM检测行为转变 + 非对称评分机制。

Result: 比特币OTC中位入度2时，战略攻击下F1上限仅0.451；二元模型保留86%互信息；消融实验发现调制机制在二元观测上提升AUC 0.030，但在连续观测上降低0.094；整体系统在比特币OTC达AUC 0.749，比特币Alpha达0.796，创始人标签AUC 0.999。

Conclusion: 检测器-模型匹配比信息含量更关键；所提双机制架构在8种攻击下均显著优于GaaSTrust（p<0.003），验证了针对离散稀疏评分网络设计的有效性。

Abstract: Sequential trust detection in rating networks relies on continuous observation models that fail on real data. On Bitcoin-OTC, 56\% of ratings take a single value under standard mapping, breaking the distributional assumptions that parametric detectors require. This paper makes three contributions. It derives a Bayes-optimal F1 detection ceiling for per-node sequential detectors using empirically measured observation parameters. At Bitcoin-OTC's median in-degree of 2, this ceiling falls to 0.451 for strategic attacks, explaining why unsupervised methods cluster near $F1 \approx 0.4$. The analysis shows that detector-model matching, not information content, determines performance: binary models retain 86\% of mutual information while enabling exact parametric fit. A dual-regime architecture is presented where Bernoulli CUSUM detects behavioral shifts and triggers asymmetric scoring. Ablation reveals a co-design constraint: the modulation mechanism improves AUC by 0.030 on binary observations but degrades it by 0.094 on continuous observations. The combined system achieves AUC 0.749 on Bitcoin-OTC and 0.796 on Bitcoin-Alpha, beating GaaSTrust on all 8 attacks ($p < 0.003$), with founder-label AUC of 0.999.

</details>


### [114] [A-Live: Passive Liveness Detection via Neuromuscular Micro-Motion Signatures on Commodity Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05126)
*Mohammed Gharib,Sam Burns,Martin Zizi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出A-Live，一种仅基于消费设备中惯性测量单元（IMU）信号的被动式活体检测框架。它利用人类神经肌肉微运动在惯性数据中产生的细微可测量特征，设计了轻量级特征提取流水线和紧凑型分类器，并引入可控物理微运动平台以评估对非人类运动的鲁棒性。在Android和iOS设备上的实验表明，A-Live能达到超过99.5%的准确率，且具备低误接受率和低误拒绝率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活体检测的需求已从传统生物认证中的演示和重放攻击防御，扩展到区分人类用户与非人类智能体。现有方法依赖显式用户交互、专用硬件、易受逼真欺骗攻击影响，且在实际部署中可扩展性有限。生成式AI和代理型AI的兴起进一步放大了这一需求，亟需一种新型活体检测方案。

Method: 基于人类运动控制中固有的神经肌肉微运动在惯性数据中产生细微可测量特征的观察，设计了轻量级特征提取管道和紧凑型分类器，在设备端实时运行；同时引入了可控物理微运动平台以模拟和评估对非人类工程化运动的鲁棒性。

Result: 在Android和iOS设备上的自动化和真实用户测试中，A-Live达到超过99.5%的准确率，且误接受率和误拒绝率均较低。

Conclusion: 神经肌肉微运动特征为新兴AI驱动威胁模型下的活体检测提供了可扩展且被动的解决方案，A-Live证明了该方法的有效性和实际部署潜力。

Abstract: Liveness detection has evolved from a safeguard against presentation and replay attacks in biometric authentication to a broader requirement for distinguishing human users from non-human agents in modern digital systems. The emergence of generative and agentic AI further amplifies this need, positioning liveness as a fundamental security primitive. Existing approaches face key limitations, including reliance on explicit user interaction, specialized hardware, vulnerability to increasingly realistic spoofing, and limited scalability in real-world deployments. We present A-Live, a passive liveness detection framework that operates solely on inertial measurement unit (IMU) signals available in commodity devices. A-Live is based on the observation that neuromuscular micro-motions inherent to human motor control produce subtle but measurable signatures in inertial data, which are often treated as noise in prior work. We design a lightweight feature extraction pipeline and a compact classifier suitable for real-time on-device deployment, and introduce a controllable physical micro-motion platform to evaluate robustness against engineered non-human motion. Extensive evaluation across Android and iOS devices, including both automated and real-user settings, shows that A-Live achieves over 99.5\% accuracy with low false acceptance and rejection rates. Our results demonstrate that neuromuscular micro-motion signatures provide a scalable and passive foundation for liveness detection under emerging AI-driven threat models.

</details>


### [115] [Preserving Data Privacy in Learning Causal Structure with Fully Homomorphic Encryption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05129)
*Jian Yang,Yuan Tong,Qinbin Li,Zeyi Wen,Xiaofang Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于全同态加密（FHE）的分布式因果结构学习方法，通过电路简化、牛顿-拉夫森倒数近似和泰勒展开的除法和对数运算逼近，以及SIMD加速的批处理技术，在保护数据隐私的同时高效完成因果结构学习，实验结果与明文版本高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分布式因果结构学习中，数据传输和计算过程中存在隐私泄露风险，现有FHE方法因计算成本高且不支持除法和对数运算而难以直接应用。

Method: (i) 电路简化以提高效率；(ii) 通过牛顿-拉夫森倒数法和泰勒展开近似除法和对数运算；(iii) SIMD加速的批处理技术。方法还支持扩展至差分隐私。

Result: 在测试数据集上，该方法与明文版本的因果结构高度一致，且能够在数十分钟内完成隐私保护下的因果结构学习。

Conclusion: 所提出的FHE方法在保证隐私安全的前提下，实现了高效且实用的分布式因果结构学习，并具备向差分隐私扩展的便携性。

Abstract: Preserving data privacy is an important topic in structural data management and data mining. However, the issue of privacy leakage in distributed causal structure learning is a persistent challenge, especially in cases where data transmission and computation are required. In this paper, we propose a method based on fully homomorphic encryption (FHE) that performs calculations on ciphertexts, keeping data encrypted in transition and computation. Nevertheless, adopting FHE to causal structure learning is challenging due to the high computation cost and limited support on division as well as logarithm operations in FHE. To tackle this challenge, we propose a series of novel techniques including (i) circuit simplification for better efficiency, (ii) approximation of division and logarithm through Newton-Raphson Reciprocal and Taylor expansion, and (iii) a batching technique with SIMD-acceleration to enhance the whole learning process. Additionally, our method can be easily extended beyond FHE by demonstration of its portability to support differential privacy. Empirical results show that our method achieves high consistency and comparable causal structure with the plaintext version in the datasets tested. Last, our method is efficient and practical to complete learning causal structures in tens of minutes even under the privacy protection of FHE.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [116] [Toward Pre-Deployment Assurance for Enterprise AI Agents: Ontology-Grounded Simulation and Trust Certification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04037)
*Thanh Luong Tuan,Abhijit Sanyal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于本体论的AI智能体验证框架，包含操作包线、本体到场景的生成管道和可信证书三部分。在四个受监管行业的试点中，本体驱动方法在监管覆盖率和领域特异性上显著优于基于角色的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业AI智能体在部署前的验证存在关键空白，现有手段（如部署后监控、人在回路控制、提示级护栏）在生产环境中的保障能力有限。

Method: 提出三组件框架：(1) 智能体操作包线，形式化定义权限、领域约束、安全属性、治理规则和自主性等级的认证空间；(2) 本体到场景生成管道，自动推导监管、操作和对抗测试场景；(3) 可信证书，携带机器可验证的证明和渐进式部署判定（批准、有条件、拒绝）。在四个受监管行业的五组实验中进行评估。

Result: 生成了1,800个场景，覆盖125条监管要求和25个注入故障。本体驱动方法(G4)达到48.3%的监管覆盖率（基线33.1%，校正p=.0006），领域特异性最高（4.77/5.0，p=2e-6）。跨三个LLM家族（共5,400场景）验证了相同模式。但相对于基线+RAP的覆盖率优势在Bonferroni校正后不显著。

Conclusion: 本体驱动场景生成可作为基于角色的测试套件在监管密集型领域的可信补充方法。

Abstract: Pre-deployment verification of enterprise artificial intelligence (AI) agents remains a critical gap between large language model (LLM) capability benchmarking and production deployment. Post-deployment monitoring, human-in-the-loop controls, and prompt-level guardrails offer limited assurance once an agent is operating in production.
